Her water bottle appeared only in the evening, so she was probably out during the day and brought it home. Her mug was in the kitchen sink and her vitamins were on the kitchen table in the morning. Her camera is on the living room bookshelf.

Shared things mostly stay put: bathroom items on the shelf or cabinet, kitchen items in the cupboard, pantry or counter, and living room items in place. Nobody was seen in the living room.

*anything changed:* I can't tell yet. This is day 1 and I have nothing earlier to compare with. During the day, the kitchen went from morning dishes to a shared dinner around 20:00 and was then tidied by about 20:54.

*why:* Nothing has been seen to change yet, so there is no reason to give. My guesses about today, with low to medium confidence: Leo works from home in the office, Aisha is out during the day (work or study), and Elena is home and handles meals and cleaning up.

*happened before:* I don't know. This is the first day I have any record of.

*expect tomorrow:* Leo: at the office desk from morning until late night, with his laptop and other things there, coming to the kitchen for dinner around 20:00. Elena: bathroom in the morning, then the kitchen at breakfast, lunch and dinner, clearing up after dinner around 21:00; her personal things stay in bedroom_2. Aisha: out during the day, back for dinner around 20:00, with her water bottle ending up in the kitchen (on the dish rack or table). Shared things should stay where they usually are. My confidence is low to medium because this is based on a single day. I don't know if tomorrow is a weekday or a weekend, and a weekend could change this.

He takes short breaks: lunch around 13:00 (plate and glass on the kitchen table, bowl in the sink) and a kitchen visit around 15:21. At 15:30 the office was empty, the charger was gone and the laptop was on the office floor. By 15:46 everything was back on the desk. At dinner around 19:56 his water bottle was on the kitchen table, and afterwards it went to the dish rack. His phone was left on the kitchen table at 21:10. At 22:59 the office was empty, the charger and water bottle were gone, and the headphones were still on the desk. His mug lives on the office desk all day.
SHARED THINGS: Everything in the bathroom and the living room stayed fixed all day. Both vitamin bottles now usually sit on the kitchen counter. The dog food bag was on a kitchen chair from dinner onward.

*anything changed:* Some timings moved compared with day 1. Leo started in the office at 09:11 (day 1: by 09:52), and Elena ate breakfast with him around 08:40 (day 1: around 10:00). Dinner was one sitting with all three around 19:56, not the two sittings I thought I saw on day 1. bowl_elena was back in the cupboard instead of on the pantry shelf. Leo's headphones stayed on the office desk overnight instead of leaving with him. None of this looks like a real change in anyone's life. Each person's overall pattern was the same as on day 1: Aisha out in the daytime, Leo in the office, Elena at home.

*why:* I see no real change in anyone's life. My best guess is that the differences are normal day-to-day variation, or that on day 1 I missed the early morning and the first dinner sitting. I'm moderately sure of this. It is only the second day I have watched.

*happened before:* Yes. Day 1 looked the same in outline: Aisha away in the daytime and back for dinner, Leo working in the office until late, and Elena at home eating in the kitchen and clearing up after dinner. It has not ended; it continued through day 2. I have only two days of history, so I can't say anything about longer cycles such as weekends.

*expect tomorrow:* If tomorrow is a working day, I expect the same as today:
- Aisha eats breakfast around 08:00, leaves around 08:30 with her water bottle and tablet, and comes back for dinner around 19:45. Her bowl and mug will be left in the sink.

- Elena eats at the kitchen table around 08:45, 13:00 and 15:30, spends the rest of the day in bedroom_2, and clears the kitchen after dinner.
If it turns out to be a weekend, Leo and Aisha may stay home and the office may be empty. I expect the weekday pattern to continue for the foreseeable future, but I have only two days of data.

## Night 4 (normal)

**Answers to the five questions (asked before writing notes):**

*routines:* Leo works from home in the office. He is in the bathroom around 07:45. He is at the office desk from about 08:30 until late at night, and I still saw him there at 22:48. He comes out for quick kitchen trips: breakfast around 08:30, with his bowl, mug, vitamins and tablet on the kitchen table; lunch around 12:45 with Elena; and dinner around 19:30 to 20:15 with everyone. His laptop, charger, notebook, pen and the plant pot stay on desk_o1. He brings his water bottle and mug to the desk while he works. Today his charger stayed on the desk through dinner. At 22:48 his headphones were on office_shelf_o1 instead of the desk. His tablet stayed on the kitchen table all day and was still there at 22:48.

Elena is home during the day, but I mostly see her in the kitchen. Her breakfast bowl and mug were still in the cupboard at 08:30 and were in the sink by 12:43, so she eats breakfast after 08:30. She has lunch with Leo around 12:45. Her plate went to the sink by 16:00, but her glass stayed on the table until the evening. She is at dinner, and her bowl, mug and glass were back in the cupboard by 22:48.

Aisha is out all day. I did not see her until dinner at 19:32. Her bowl, mug, plate and glass stayed in the cupboard all day, so she either skipped breakfast or washed up before 08:30. She came home with her water bottle: it was on the kitchen table at 20:05 and on dish_rack_k1 at 22:48. Her vitamins stay on the counter.

Shared things: nothing in the bathroom moved all day. Aisha's towel is on the shelf, and Elena's and Leo's towels are on the rack. The dog food bag stayed on pantry_shelf_k1 every time I looked.

*anything changed:* Nothing big changed. All three kept the same shape of day as on days 1 to 3. There were small differences from my notes:
- Leo was at his desk by 08:30, earlier than the 09:52 I had noted before.
- Leo's charger stayed on the desk through dinner. My note said it leaves at dinner.
- Leo's headphones moved to the office shelf at night. My note said they never move.
- Leo's phone was not on the bathroom shelf in the morning.
- Leo's tablet stayed on the kitchen table overnight instead of going to dresser_b1.
- Leo's water bottle was put away in the cupboard at night, not on the dish rack.
- Aisha's water bottle stayed on the dish rack and did not go to bedroom_3.
- The dog food bag never went to chair_k1.
- At 22:48 the late clear-up was not finished: Elena's and Leo's plates, Leo's glass and the snack bowl were still in the sink.
- The shopping bag was missing at 19:32 and back on the pantry shelf by 20:05.
- My sightings disagree with each other about mug_leo. The kitchen looks show it in the sink (12:43, 15:58, 19:32, 20:05), but the office looks show it on the desk (13:38, 16:54, 22:48). It may be two mugs, or I may have misread one of them.

*why:* I don't see a real change in anyone's life. The small differences look like ordinary day-to-day variation: Leo starting work a little earlier, someone tidying a bit differently, and the late washing-up left for the morning. I am fairly sure of this, about 75%. The shopping bag being gone briefly around 19:30 may mean someone (perhaps Aisha on her way home) did some shopping, but I am not sure. I can't explain the double sighting of Leo's mug. I suspect a perception error, or a second mug that looks the same.

*happened before:* Yes. Today matches days 1 to 3: Leo working in the office all day, Elena at home and in the kitchen at meals, Aisha out until dinner, and dinner together around 19:30 to 20:30. Unfinished clearing-up has happened before. On day 2 at 21:10 cooking things were still in the sink, and on day 1 breakfast dishes sat in the sink until the evening. Both times things were put away later. I have not seen a pattern start and then end, so I can't say how one would end.

*expect tomorrow:* If tomorrow is another working day, I expect:
- Leo: in the bathroom around 07:45, then at desk_o1 from about 08:30 into the late evening, with his laptop, charger, notebook and pen on the desk. Breakfast in the kitchen around 08:30, lunch around 12:45 and dinner around 19:30.
- Elena: breakfast after 08:30, lunch with Leo around 12:45, and handling the kitchen after dinner. The plates left in the sink tonight will probably be washed by mid-morning or the early afternoon.
- Aisha: out from early morning until about 19:30, taking her water bottle with her, then dinner with the others.

I expect this pattern to hold for the rest of the working week. On a weekend day it could break, with Leo away from the office and Aisha at home during the day.
